get_nat_plan_rebels = {

	get_planetary_reb_number = yes

	random_country = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = nat_motherland_sit_@root
			has_country_flag = nat_motherland_sit_@root.owner
		}
		save_event_target_as = nat_motherland_model
		set_country_flag = nat_planet_@prev
		if = {
			limit = { is_exile_country = yes }
			prev.owner = { set_country_flag = motherland_is_exile }
		}
	}

	if = { # New country has the same flag and name if it is a restoration
		limit = {
			event_target:nat_motherland_model = { is_exile_country = yes }
			NOT = {
				any_playable_country = {
					has_country_flag = exile_link_@event_target:nat_motherland_model
				}
			}
		}
		create_rebels = {
			name = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			species = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			authority = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			ethos = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			origin = origin_nationalist_rebellion
			civics = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			# flag = event_target:nat_motherland_model # Was causing things to break weirdly, try again in future
			flag = random
			effect = {
				set_country_flag = nat_country
				set_country_flag = exile_restored_country
				set_country_flag = exile_link_@event_target:nat_motherland_model
				created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = yes
			}
		}
		event_target:nat_motherland_model = { set_country_flag = exile_link_@event_target:civwar_rebels }
	}
	else = {
		create_rebels = {
			name = random
			species = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			authority = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			ethos = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			origin = origin_nationalist_rebellion
			civics = event_target:nat_motherland_model
			flag = random
			effect = {
				set_country_flag = nat_country
				created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}

	last_created_country = { set_country_flag = nat_civwar }
}

get_plan_slave_rebels = {

	save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
	save_event_target_as = civil_war_slave_leader_planet
	set_carrier_flag = plan_slave_revolt
	set_timed_carrier_flag = { flag = recent_slave_rebellion years = 5 }

	get_planetary_slave_reb_number = yes

	every_owned_species = {
		limit = {
			any_species_pop_group = {
				is_enslaved = yes
				planet = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = species_pop_count value = 0 }
		every_species_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				is_enslaved = yes
				planet = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet }
			}
			prev = { change_variable = { which = species_pop_count value = 1 } }
		}
	}

	ordered_owned_species = {
		limit = { is_variable_set = species_pop_count }
		position = 0
		order_by = species_pop_count
		save_event_target_as = civwar_species
	}

	random_list = {
		40 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = democratic }
		}
		15 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_dict_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_militarist ETH_2 = ethic_egalitarian }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = dict_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_ecaf_mod }
		}
		45 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_strict_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_fanatic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = strict_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_ecaf_mod }
		}
		50 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_bugged_direct_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_fanatic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = bugged_direct_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_bug_branch }
		}
		50 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_direct_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_fanatic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = direct_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_ecc_classic }
		}
	}

	every_owned_species = {
		limit = { is_variable_set = species_pop_count }
		clear_variable = species_pop_count
	}
}

get_sec_slave_rebels = {

	save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
	set_carrier_flag = sec_slave_revolt
	set_timed_carrier_flag = { flag = recent_sector_slave_rebellion years = 5 }

	sector = {
		save_event_target_as = sector_name_local
		set_sector_flag = sec_slave_revolt_@prev
		every_owned_planet = {
			every_owned_species = {
				limit = {
					any_species_pop_group = {
						is_enslaved = yes
						planet = { is_same_value = prevprevprev }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { which = species_pop_count value = 0 }
				every_species_pop_group = {
					limit = {
						is_enslaved = yes
						planet = { is_same_value = prevprevprev }
					}
					prev = { change_variable = { which = species_pop_count value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}

		owner = {
			ordered_owned_species = {
				limit = { is_variable_set = species_pop_count }
				position = 0
				order_by = species_pop_count
				save_event_target_as = civwar_species
			}
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = { any_owned_species = { is_same_species = event_target:civwar_species } }
				save_event_target_as = civil_war_slave_leader_planet
			}
		}
	}

	create_sector_name_leader = yes

	get_sectoral_slave_reb_number = yes

	random_list = {
		40 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = democratic }
		}
		15 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_dict_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_militarist ETH_2 = ethic_egalitarian }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = dict_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_ecaf_mod }
		}
		45 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_strict_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_fanatic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = strict_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_ecaf_mod }
		}
		50 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_bugged_direct_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_fanatic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = bugged_direct_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_bug_branch }
		}
		50 = {
			create_rebels = {
				authority = auth_direct_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/slave_rebels ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_egalitarian ETH_2 = ethic_fanatic_militarist }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/authority_doubler_modifier AUTH = direct_democratic }
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/modded_auth_check MOD = has_ecc_classic }
		}
	}

	every_owned_species = {
		limit = { is_variable_set = species_pop_count }
		clear_variable = species_pop_count
	}
}

#### Ethic-based Countries
create_ethic_country = {
	optimize_memory
	random_list = {
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/democratic_modifiers }
		}
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_imperial
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/imperial_modifiers }
		}
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_dictatorial
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/dictatorial_modifiers }
		}
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_oligarchic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/oligarchic_modifiers }
		}
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_corporate
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/corporate_modifiers }
		}
		###########
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_strict_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/strict_democratic_modifiers }
		}
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_dict_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/dict_democratic_modifiers }
		}
		###########
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_bugged_direct_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/bugged_direct_democratic_modifiers }
		}
		###########
		0 = {
			create_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ = {
				authority = auth_direct_democratic
				inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/gen_$COUNTRY_REBELS$ ETH_1 = ethic_fanatic_$FAN_ETH$ ETH_2 = random }
			}
			inline_script = { script = modifiers/direct_democratic_modifiers }
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:civwar_party
			event_target:civwar_party = { is_pop_faction_type = supremacist }
			event_target:civwar_originator = { has_valid_civic = civic_fanatic_purifiers }
		}
		last_created_country = { force_add_civic = civic_fanatic_purifiers }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:civwar_party
			event_target:civwar_party = { is_pop_faction_type = supremacist }
			last_created_country = { num_species = 1 }
			last_created_country = { is_spiritualist = yes }
			last_created_country = { NOT = { has_valid_civic = civic_fanatic_purifiers } }
		}
		last_created_country = { random = { chance = 45 force_add_civic = civic_fanatic_purifiers } }
	}
}

##########

created_civwar_country_effect = {
	establish_communications_no_message = root # If this isnt done here, a message will still pop up as of 3.5
	set_timed_country_flag = { flag = civwar_rebel_timer months = 3 }
	save_event_target_as = civwar_rebels
	log = "ORIGIN: \\[event_target:civwar_originator.GetName]"
	log = "COUNTRY EFFECT"
	country_event = { id = civwar.1000 }
}

created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = {
	optimize_memory
	set_timed_country_flag = { flag = civwar_rebel_timer months = 3 }
	save_event_target_as = civwar_rebels
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = event_target:cw_scope_initializer
			event_target:cw_scope_initializer = {
				is_scope_type = pop_faction
				is_regular_faction = yes
			}
		}
		event_target:cw_scope_initializer = {
			switch = {
				trigger = has_ethic
				ethic_egalitarian = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = egalitarian } } }
				ethic_authoritarian = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = authoritarian } } }
				ethic_spiritualist = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = spiritualist } } }
				ethic_materialist = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = materialist } } }
				ethic_militarist = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = militarist } } }
				ethic_pacifist = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = pacifist } } }
				ethic_xenophile = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = xenophile } } }
				ethic_xenophobe = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = xenophobe } } }
				ethic_socialism = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = socialism } } }
				ethic_capitalism = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = capitalism } } }
				ethic_cooperative = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = cooperative } } }
				ethic_competitive = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = competitive } } }
				ethic_industrial = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = industrial } } }
				ethic_green = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = green } } }
				ethic_anthropocentric = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = anthropocentric } } }
				ethic_ecocentric = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = ecocentric } } }
				ethic_elitist = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = elitist } } }
				ethic_pluralist = { owner = { inline_script = { script = effects/created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic ETHIC = pluralist } } }

				# ethic_egalitarian = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = egalitarian } } }
				# ethic_authoritarian = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = authoritarian } } }
				# ethic_spiritualist = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = spiritualist } } }
				# ethic_materialist = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = materialist } } }
				# ethic_militarist = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = militarist } } }
				# ethic_pacifist = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = pacifist } } }
				# ethic_xenophile = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = xenophile } } }
				# ethic_xenophobe = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = xenophobe } } }
				# ethic_socialism = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = socialism } } }
				# ethic_capitalism = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = capitalism } } }
				# ethic_cooperative = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = cooperative } } }
				# ethic_competitive = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = competitive } } }
				# ethic_industrial = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = industrial } } }
				# ethic_green = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = green } } }
				# ethic_anthropocentric = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = anthropocentric } } }
				# ethic_ecocentric = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = ecocentric } } }
				# ethic_elitist = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = elitist } } }
				# ethic_pluralist = { owner = { created_civwar_rebel_leader_ethic = { ETHIC = pluralist } } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				exists = event_target:new_rebel_leader
				has_origin = origin_nationalist_rebellion
				has_origin = origin_civstrife_slave_rebellion
			}
			event_target:civil_war_planet.owner = {
				any_owned_leader = {
					OR = {
						background_planet = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet }
						AND = {
							event_target:civil_war_planet = { has_carrier_flag = sep_sector_rebellion }
							exists = background_planet.sector
							background_planet.sector = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet.sector }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		while = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = event_target:new_rebel_leader }
				any_owned_leader = {
					NOT = { has_leader_flag = homeworld_leader_checked }
					OR = {
						background_planet = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet }
						AND = {
							event_target:civil_war_planet = { has_carrier_flag = sep_sector_rebellion }
							exists = background_planet.sector
							background_planet.sector = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet.sector }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ordered_owned_leader = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_leader_flag = homeworld_leader_checked }
					OR = {
						background_planet = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet }
						AND = {
							event_target:civil_war_planet = { has_carrier_flag = sep_sector_rebellion }
							exists = background_planet.sector
							background_planet.sector = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet.sector }
						}
					}
				}
				position = 0
				order_by = trigger:leader_age
				inverse = yes
				## ( 100 / (Age + Yrs Experience)) * 20 = Percentage Chance of Leader leading rebellion
				set_variable = { which = rebel_leader_chance value = 100 }
				set_variable = { which = rebel_leader_age_experience value = trigger:leader_age }
				change_variable = { which = rebel_leader_age_experience value = trigger:leader_years_of_service }
				divide_variable = { which = rebel_leader_chance value = rebel_leader_age_experience }
				multiply_variable = { which = rebel_leader_chance value = 20 }
				set_leader_flag = homeworld_leader_checked
				random = {
					chance = rebel_leader_chance
					save_event_target_as = new_rebel_leader
					species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
				}
				clear_variable = rebel_leader_chance
				clear_variable = rebel_leader_age_experience
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = event_target:new_rebel_leader } }
		create_leader = {
			class = random_ruler
			species = event_target:civwar_species
			name = random
			skill = 5
			background_planet = event_target:civil_war_planet
			effect = {
				set_leader_flag = new_civwar_ruler
				save_event_target_as = new_rebel_leader
			}
		}
	}

	event_target:new_rebel_leader = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_leader_flag = new_civwar_ruler } }
			set_defected_real = yes ### Add_trait will break if nested inside 1 more scripted effect from here, so use _real
		}
	}
	set_leader = event_target:new_rebel_leader # Needs to be after the above with the leader flags

	event_target:civil_war_planet = {
		rebel_army_effect = yes
	}
}

created_slave_rebel_country_effect = {
	set_country_flag = slave_rebels
	save_event_target_as = rebelling_slaves
	establish_communications_no_message = root.owner
	set_relation_flag = {
		flag = rebelling_slaves_former_owner
		who = root.owner
	}
	create_leader = {
		class = random_ruler
		species = event_target:civwar_species
		name = random
		skill = 5
		background_planet = event_target:civil_war_slave_leader_planet
		effect = {
			set_leader_flag = new_civwar_ruler
			save_event_target_as = new_rebel_leader
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = has_ALTM_mod } }
				add_trait = {
					trait = trait_ruler_charismatic_2
					show_message = no
				}
				add_trait = {
					trait = trait_ruler_champion_of_the_people_2
					show_message = no
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_charismatic_2
					show_message = no
				}
				add_trait = {
					trait = leader_trait_champion_of_ppl_2
					show_message = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
	created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = yes
}

#####################
# Civil War Type Effects

create_civil_war = {
	optimize_memory
	log = "--------------------------------"
	log = "| Create Civil War Effect Called - \\[GetDate]"
	owner = {
		save_event_target_as = civwar_originator
		log = "| ORIGINATOR: \\[this.GetName]"
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_scope_type = pop_faction
				is_scope_type = country
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { is_scope_type = pop_faction }
			log = "| Faction Civil War"
			log = "| Faction: \\[this.GetName]"
			save_event_target_as = civwar_party
			save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
		}
		else = {
			log = "| Country Civil War"
		}
		
		owner = {
			if = { # 2 Sectors, other Sector will revolt
				limit = {
					num_sectors = 2
					any_owned_sector = {
						NOR = {
							any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								OR = {
									is_capital = yes
									has_ground_combat = yes
									is_occupied_flag = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				log = "| 2 Sector Civwar"

				if = {
					limit = { root = { is_scope_type = country } }
					random_pop_faction = {
						limit = { is_regular_faction = yes }
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
						random_owned_pop_group = { species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species } }
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						any_owned_pop_group = {
							pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							sector = { NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } } }
						}
					}
					random_owned_pop_group = {
						limit = {
							pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							sector = { NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } } }
						}
						species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
					}
				}
				else = {
					random_owned_sector = {
						limit = { NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } } }
						random_owned_pop_group = { species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species } }
					}
				}

				set_recent_civwar_flag = yes

				create_party_name_leader = yes

				random_owned_sector = {
					limit = { NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } } }
					set_sector_flag = leader_sector
					make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = { # > 2 Sectors, if applicable, up to 67% can revolt
				limit = {
					num_sectors > 2
					any_owned_sector = {
						NOR = {
							any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								OR = {
									is_capital = yes
									has_ground_combat = yes
									is_occupied_flag = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				log = "| Multi Sector Civwar"

				if = {
					limit = { root = { is_scope_type = country } }
					random_pop_faction = {
						limit = { is_regular_faction = yes }
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
					}
				}

				export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
					trigger = num_sectors
					variable = all_potential_sectors
				}

				set_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_sectors value = 0 }

				every_owned_sector = { # Calculate the rebel percentage of each sector
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								OR = {
									is_capital = yes
									has_ground_combat = yes
									is_occupied_flag = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
					set_sector_flag = potential_rebel
					export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
						trigger = pop_amount
						variable = total_sector_pops
					}
					set_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 0 }
					every_owned_planet = {
						every_owned_pop_group = {
							limit = {
								exists = pop_faction
								pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							}
							prevprev = { change_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 1 } }
						}
					}
					divide_variable = {
						which = fac_sep_percentage
						value = total_sector_pops
					}
					multiply_variable = { # Needs to be done to ensure accurate ordering
						which = fac_sep_percentage
						value = 100
					}
					prev = { change_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_sectors value = 1 } }
				}

				random_list = {
					21 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.3 } }
					31 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.4 } }
					46 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.5 } }
					2 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.67 } }
				}
				ceiling_variable = all_potential_sectors

				while = { # Pick the most separatist sectors to rebel
					count = all_potential_sectors
					ordered_owned_sector = {
						limit = {
							has_sector_flag = potential_rebel
							NOT = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector }
						}
						position = 0
						order_by = fac_sep_percentage
						set_sector_flag = rebel_sector
					}
				}

				while = { # Associate bordering sectors with each other
					count = all_potential_sectors
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_owned_sector = {
								has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
								NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
							}
						}
						ordered_owned_sector = {
							limit = {
								has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
								NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
							}
							position = 0
							order_by = fac_sep_percentage
							save_event_target_as = rebel_sector_leader
							set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@this
							set_sector_flag = leader_sector
							set_sector_flag = counted_sector
						}
						while = {
							count = all_potential_sectors
							if = { # Any Sector that borders a more rebellious sector will rebel under them
								limit = {
									any_owned_sector = {
										NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
										has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
										any_system_within_border = {
											closest_system = {
												limit = {
													exists = sector
													sector = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
												}
												use_bypasses = yes
												max_steps = 1
											}
										}
									}
								}
								random_owned_sector = {
									limit = {
										NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
										has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
										any_system_within_border = {
											closest_system = {
												limit = {
													exists = sector
													sector = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
												}
												use_bypasses = yes
												max_steps = 1
											}
										}
									}
									set_sector_flag = counted_sector
									set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
									set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_follower_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}

				set_recent_civwar_flag = yes

				# I Imagine this should stay past the first loop
				event_target:civwar_party = {
					save_event_target_as = civwar_party
					random_owned_pop_group = { species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species } }
				}

				create_party_name_leader = yes

				set_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value = 0 }
				every_owned_sector = {
					limit = { has_sector_flag = leader_sector }
					prev = { change_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value = 1 } }
				}

				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value > 1 } }
					set_timed_country_flag = { flag = rebs_help_eachother months = 2 }
					random = { ## For multiple disparate sectors, this needs to be handled differently
						chance = value:total_war_chance
						ordered_owned_sector = {
							limit = { has_sector_flag = leader_sector }
							position = 0
							order_by = fac_sep_percentage
							set_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector
							save_event_target_as = rebel_sector_leader
						}
						every_owned_sector = { ## Get rid of other leader sectors
							limit = {
								has_sector_flag = leader_sector
								NOT = { has_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector }
							}
							remove_sector_flag = leader_sector
						}
						every_owned_sector = {
							limit = {
								NOT = { has_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector }
								has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
							}
							set_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector
							set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
							set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_follower_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
						}
						event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { set_variable = { which = multi_sector_TW_trigger value = 1 } }
						make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { is_variable_set = multi_sector_TW_trigger } }
					event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { clear_variable = multi_sector_TW_trigger }
				}
				else = {
					event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { set_variable = { which = disallow_total_war value = 1 } } ## This is necessary so that the second/third/etc rebel country does not turn into a total civil war country
					while = {
						count = total_reb_countries
						ordered_owned_sector = {
							limit = { has_sector_flag = leader_sector }
							position = 0
							order_by = fac_sep_percentage
							event_target:civwar_party = {
								random_owned_pop_group = {
									limit = { sector = { is_same_value = prevprevprev } }
									species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
								}
							}
							make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
						}
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value > 1 } }
							last_created_country = { set_timed_country_flag = { flag = allied_via_@event_target:cw_scope_initializer.owner months = 2 } }
						}
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value > 1 } }
					last_created_country = { set_timed_country_flag = { flag = allied_to_others months = 6 } }
				}
				
				every_owned_sector = {
					limit = { has_sector_flag = potential_rebel }
					remove_sector_flag = potential_rebel
				}

				event_target:cw_scope_initializer = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_variable_set = disallow_total_war }
						clear_variable = disallow_total_war
					}
				}
				
			}
			else_if = { # >= 2 Planets
				limit = {
					num_owned_planets >= 2
					any_owned_planet = {
						pop_amount > 0
						is_capital = no
						solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
						has_ground_combat = no
						is_occupied_flag = no
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { root = { is_scope_type = pop_faction } }
					root = { set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 } }
				}
				else = {
					random_pop_faction = {
						limit = { is_regular_faction = yes }
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
						set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 }
					}
				}

				if = { # If only 2 planets, get the other planet
					limit = { num_owned_planets = 2 }
					random_owned_planet = {
						limit = {
							pop_amount > 0
							is_capital = no
							solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
							has_ground_combat = no
							is_occupied_flag = no
						}
						save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
						solar_system = { set_timed_star_flag = { flag = civwar_planet months = 3 } }
						every_owned_pop_group = {
							limit = {
								exists = pop_faction
								pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							}
							event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
						}
						random_owned_pop_group = {
							limit = {
								exists = pop_faction
								pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							}
							species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
						}
						set_carrier_flag = small_civil_war
						get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
					}
				}
				else = { # If more than 2, potentially Balkanize
					set_variable = { which = total_happiness_pops value = 0 }
					every_owned_pop_group = {
						limit = { pop_group_has_happiness = yes }
						prev = { change_variable = { which = total_happiness_pops value = 1 } }
					}
					set_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_planets value = 0 }
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								is_capital = no
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
								pop_amount_percentage = {
									percentage >= @civwar_pop_percent_needed
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									}
								}
							}
						}
						every_owned_planet = {
							limit = {
								pop_amount >= 0
								is_capital = no
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
								pop_amount_percentage = {
									percentage >= @civwar_pop_percent_needed
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									}
								}
							}
							set_timed_carrier_flag = { flag = maybe_balkan_war days = 30 }
							prev = { change_variable = { which = yes_rebel_planets value = 1 } }
						}
					}
					else = {
						ordered_owned_planet = {
							limit = {
								pop_amount >= 0
								is_capital = no
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
								pop_amount_percentage = {
									percentage >= 0
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									}
								}
							}
							position = 0
							order_by = trigger:pop_amount
							set_timed_carrier_flag = { flag = maybe_balkan_war days = 30 }
							prev = { change_variable = { which = yes_rebel_planets value = 1 } }
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = yes_rebel_planets
						ordered_owned_planet = {
							limit = { has_carrier_flag = maybe_balkan_war }
							position = 0
							order_by = trigger:pop_amount

							save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
							solar_system = { set_timed_star_flag = { flag = civwar_planet months = 3 } }
							every_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = {
									exists = pop_faction
									pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
								}
								event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
							}
							random_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = {
									exists = pop_faction
									pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
								}
								species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
							}

							set_carrier_flag = balkan_war
							set_carrier_flag = ethic_planet_rebellion_@this # Such that Balkanization doesnt result in a bunch of wars numbered different civil wars at the same time
							get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
							set_recent_civwar_flag = yes
						}
					}
				}
				root = { country_event = { id = civethic.9001 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_scope_type = sector }
		log = "| Sector Civil War"
		log = "| Sector: \\[this.GetName]"
		save_event_target_as = saved_sector

		if = {
			limit = { exists = root.target }
			root.target = {
				save_event_target_as = civwar_party
				save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
				set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 }
			}

			every_owned_planet = {
				every_owned_pop_group = {
					limit = {
						exists = pop_faction
						pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
						rebel_happiness = yes
					}
					event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			random_owned_planet = {
				random_owned_pop_group = {
					limit = {
						exists = pop_faction
						pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
						rebel_happiness = yes
					}
					species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			owner = {
				random_pop_faction = {
					limit = {
						is_regular_faction = yes
						any_owned_pop_group = { sector = { is_same_value = event_target:saved_sector } }
					}
					save_event_target_as = civwar_party
					set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 }
				}
			}

			every_owned_planet = {
				every_owned_pop_group = {
					limit = {
						exists = pop_faction
						pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
					}
					event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			random_owned_planet = {
				random_owned_pop_group = {
					limit = {
						exists = pop_faction
						pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
					}
					species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
				}
			}
		}
		
		sector_capital = {
			save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
			owner = { create_party_name_leader = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
				set_carrier_flag = ethic_sector_rebellion
				get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
				set_recent_civwar_flag = yes
				sector = { set_sector_flag = ethic_sector_rebellion_@event_target:civil_war_planet }
			}
			else = {
				set_carrier_flag = nat_sector_rebellion
				get_nat_plan_rebels = yes
				sector = { set_sector_flag = nat_sector_rebellion_@event_target:civil_war_planet }
			}
		}
		
		save_event_target_as = sector_name_local
		
		create_sector_name_leader = yes # Create a dummy leader to save the sector name for localisation

		if = {
			limit = { exists = root.target }
			root = {
				situation_event = { id = civethic.9010 }
				if = {
					limit = { target = { is_regular_faction = no } }
					target = {
						every_owned_pop_group = {
							limit = { has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat }
							random = {
								chance = 40
								remove_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			owner = { country_event = { id = civethic.9011 } }
		}
		
		
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_scope_type = planet }
		log = "| Planetary Rebellion"
		log = "| Planet: \\[this.GetName]"
		get_planetary_reb_number = yes
		save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
		solar_system = { set_timed_star_flag = { flag = civwar_planet months = 3 } }

		if = {
			limit = { exists = root.target }
			root.target = {
				set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 }
				save_event_target_as = civwar_party
				save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
			}
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					exists = pop_faction
					pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
					rebel_happiness = yes
				}
				event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
			}
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					exists = pop_faction
					pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
					rebel_happiness = yes
				}
				species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
			}
		}
		else = {
			owner = {
				random_pop_faction = {
					limit = { any_owned_pop_group = { planet = { is_same_value = event_target:civil_war_planet } } }
					set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 }
					save_event_target_as = civwar_party
					save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
				}
			}
			
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					exists = pop_faction
					pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
				}
				event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
			}
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					exists = pop_faction
					pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
				}
				species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
			set_carrier_flag = ethic_planet_rebellion_@this
			owner = { create_party_name_leader = yes }
			get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
			set_recent_civwar_flag = yes
			root = { situation_event = { id = civethic.9000 } }
		}
		else = {
			set_carrier_flag = nat_planet_rebellion_@this
			owner = { create_party_name_leader = yes }
			get_nat_plan_rebels = yes
			root = {
				situation_event = { id = civnat.9010 }
				target = {
					every_owned_pop_group = {
						limit = { has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat }
						random = {
							chance = 80
							remove_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_scope_type = leader }
		log = "| Leader Civil War"
		log = "| Leader: \\[this.GetName]"
		save_event_target_as = cw_war_leader
		set_timed_leader_flag = { flag = personal_civil_war months = 5 }
		owner = {
			if = { # 2 Sectors, other Sector will revolt
				limit = {
					num_sectors = 2
					any_owned_sector = {
						NOR = {
							any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								OR = {
									is_capital = yes
									has_ground_combat = yes
									is_occupied_flag = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				log = "| 2 Sector Civwar"

				root.species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
				if = {
					limit = { any_pop_faction = { has_same_ethics = { SCOPE1 = this SCOPE2 = event_target:cw_war_leader } } }
					ordered_pop_faction = {
						limit = { has_same_ethics = { SCOPE1 = this SCOPE2 = event_target:cw_war_leader } }
						position = 0
						order_by = trigger:pop_amount
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
					}
				}
				else = {
					event_target:cw_war_leader = {
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
					}
				}

				random_owned_sector = {
					limit = { NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } } }
					set_sector_flag = leader_sector
					make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = { # > 2 Sectors, if applicable, up to 67% can revolt
				limit = {
					num_sectors > 2
					any_owned_sector = {
						NOR = {
							any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								OR = {
									is_capital = yes
									has_ground_combat = yes
									is_occupied_flag = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				log = "| Multi Sector Civwar"

				root.species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
				if = {
					limit = { any_pop_faction = { has_same_ethics = { SCOPE1 = this SCOPE2 = event_target:cw_war_leader } } }
					ordered_pop_faction = {
						limit = { has_same_ethics = { SCOPE1 = this SCOPE2 = event_target:cw_war_leader } }
						position = 0
						order_by = trigger:pop_amount
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
					}
				}
				else = {
					event_target:cw_war_leader = {
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
					}
				}

				export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
					trigger = num_sectors
					variable = all_potential_sectors
				}

				set_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_sectors value = 0 }

				every_owned_sector = { # Calculate the rebel percentage of each sector
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								OR = {
									is_capital = yes
									has_ground_combat = yes
									is_occupied_flag = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
					set_sector_flag = potential_rebel
					export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
						trigger = pop_amount
						variable = total_sector_pops
					}
					set_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 0 }
					if = {
						limit = { exists = event_target:civwar_party }
						every_owned_planet = {
							every_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = {
									exists = pop_faction
									pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
								}
								prevprev = { change_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 1 } }
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						every_owned_planet = {
							every_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = { has_same_ethics = { SCOPE1 = this SCOPE2 = event_target:cw_scope_initializer } }
								prevprev = { change_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 1 } }
							}
						}
					}
					
					divide_variable = {
						which = fac_sep_percentage
						value = total_sector_pops
					}
					multiply_variable = { # Needs to be done to ensure accurate ordering
						which = fac_sep_percentage
						value = 100
					}
					prev = { change_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_sectors value = 1 } }
				}

				random_list = {
					21 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.3 } }
					31 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.4 } }
					46 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.5 } }
					2 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.67 } }
				}
				ceiling_variable = all_potential_sectors

				while = { # Pick the most separatist sectors to rebel
					count = all_potential_sectors
					ordered_owned_sector = {
						limit = {
							has_sector_flag = potential_rebel
							NOT = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector }
						}
						position = 0
						order_by = fac_sep_percentage
						set_sector_flag = rebel_sector
					}
				}

				while = { # Associate bordering sectors with each other
					count = all_potential_sectors
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_owned_sector = {
								has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
								NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
							}
						}
						ordered_owned_sector = {
							limit = {
								has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
								NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
							}
							position = 0
							order_by = fac_sep_percentage
							save_event_target_as = rebel_sector_leader
							set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@this
							set_sector_flag = leader_sector
							set_sector_flag = counted_sector
						}
						while = {
							count = all_potential_sectors
							if = { # Any Sector that borders a more rebellious sector will rebel under them
								limit = {
									any_owned_sector = {
										NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
										has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
										any_system_within_border = {
											closest_system = {
												limit = {
													exists = sector
													sector = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
												}
												use_bypasses = yes
												max_steps = 1
											}
										}
									}
								}
								random_owned_sector = {
									limit = {
										NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
										has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
										any_system_within_border = {
											closest_system = {
												limit = {
													exists = sector
													sector = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
												}
												use_bypasses = yes
												max_steps = 1
											}
										}
									}
									set_sector_flag = counted_sector
									set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
									set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_follower_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}

				# I Imagine this should stay past the first loop
				if = {
					limit = { exists = event_target:civwar_party }
					event_target:civwar_party = {
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						random_owned_pop_group = { species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species } }
					}
				}

				random = { ## For multiple disparate sectors, this needs to be handled differently
					chance = value:total_war_chance
					ordered_owned_sector = {
						limit = { has_sector_flag = leader_sector }
						position = 0
						order_by = fac_sep_percentage
						set_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector
						save_event_target_as = rebel_sector_leader
					}
					every_owned_sector = { ## Get rid of other leader sectors
						limit = {
							has_sector_flag = leader_sector
							NOT = { has_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector }
						}
						remove_sector_flag = leader_sector
					}
					every_owned_sector = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector }
							has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
						}
						set_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector
						set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
						set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_follower_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
					}
					event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { set_variable = { which = multi_sector_TW_trigger value = 1 } }
				}

				## Only 1 rebel for leader Civil Wars
				make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
				
				every_owned_sector = {
					limit = { has_sector_flag = potential_rebel }
					remove_sector_flag = potential_rebel
				}

				event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { clear_variable = multi_sector_TW_trigger }
			}
			else_if = { # >= 2 Planets
				limit = {
					num_owned_planets >= 2
					any_owned_planet = {
						pop_amount > 0
						solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
						has_ground_combat = no
						is_occupied_flag = no
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = { any_pop_faction = { has_same_ethics = { SCOPE1 = this SCOPE2 = event_target:cw_war_leader } } }
					ordered_pop_faction = {
						limit = { has_same_ethics = { SCOPE1 = this SCOPE2 = event_target:cw_war_leader } }
						position = 0
						order_by = trigger:pop_amount
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
						set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 }
					}
				}
				else = {
					event_target:cw_war_leader = {
						save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
					}
				}

				if = { # If only 2 planets, get the other planet
					limit = { num_owned_planets = 2 }
					random_owned_planet = {
						limit = {
							pop_amount > 0
							solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
							has_ground_combat = no
							is_occupied_flag = no
						}
						save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
						solar_system = { set_timed_star_flag = { flag = civwar_planet months = 3 } }
						every_owned_pop_group = {
							limit = {
								exists = pop_faction
								pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							}
							event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
						}
						random_owned_pop_group = {
							limit = {
								exists = pop_faction
								pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							}
							species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
						}
						set_carrier_flag = small_civil_war
						get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
					}
				}
				else = { # If more than 2, potentially Balkanize
					set_variable = { which = total_happiness_pops value = 0 }
					every_owned_pop_group = {
						limit = { pop_group_has_happiness = yes }
						prev = { change_variable = { which = total_happiness_pops value = 1 } }
					}
					set_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_planets value = 0 }
					if = {
						limit = {
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
								pop_amount_percentage = {
									percentage >= @civwar_pop_percent_needed
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									}
								}
							}
						}
						every_owned_planet = {
							limit = {
								pop_amount >= 0
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
								pop_amount_percentage = {
									percentage >= @civwar_pop_percent_needed
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									}
								}
							}
							set_timed_carrier_flag = { flag = maybe_balkan_war days = 30 }
							prev = { change_variable = { which = yes_rebel_planets value = 1 } }
						}
					}
					else = {
						ordered_owned_planet = {
							limit = {
								pop_amount >= 0
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
								pop_amount_percentage = {
									percentage >= 0
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									}
								}
							}
							position = 0
							order_by = trigger:pop_amount
							set_timed_carrier_flag = { flag = maybe_balkan_war days = 30 }
							prev = { change_variable = { which = yes_rebel_planets value = 1 } }
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = yes_rebel_planets
						ordered_owned_planet = {
							limit = { has_carrier_flag = maybe_balkan_war }
							position = 0
							order_by = trigger:pop_amount

							save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
							solar_system = { set_timed_star_flag = { flag = civwar_planet months = 3 } }
							every_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = {
									exists = pop_faction
									pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
								}
								event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
							}
							random_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = {
									exists = pop_faction
									pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
								}
								species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
							}

							set_carrier_flag = balkan_war
							set_carrier_flag = ethic_planet_rebellion_@this # Such that Balkanization doesnt result in a bunch of wars numbered different civil wars at the same time
							get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
							# set_recent_civwar_flag = yes
						}
					}
				}
				root.owner = { country_event = { id = civethic.9001 } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = { ### This one can hopefully be very versatile
		limit = { is_scope_type = situation }
		log = "| Situation Civil War"
		log = "| Situation: \\[this.GetName]"
		log = "| TARGET: \\[this.target.GetName]"
		log = "| ROOT: \\[root.GetName]"
		owner = { get_war_type_flag = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { target = { is_scope_type = pop_faction } }
			# limit = { root.target = { is_scope_type = pop_faction } }
			target = {
			# root.target = {
				save_event_target_as = civwar_party
				save_event_target_as = cw_scope_initializer
			}
			log = "| Faction: \\[cw_scope_initializer.GetName]"
			owner = {
				save_event_target_as = civwar_originator
				if = { # 2 Sectors, other Sector will revolt
					limit = {
						num_sectors = 2
						any_owned_sector = {
							NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } }
							any_owned_planet = {
								pop_amount > 0
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
							}
						}
					}
					log = "| 2 Sector Civwar"

					if = {
						limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
						set_recent_civwar_flag = yes
					}

					event_target:civwar_party = {
						random_owned_pop_group = { species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species } }
					}

					create_party_name_leader = yes

					random_owned_sector = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								any_owned_planet = {
									OR = {
										is_capital = yes
										is_occupied_flag = yes
										has_ground_combat = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						set_sector_flag = leader_sector
						if = {
							limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
							make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
						}
						else = {
							make_nat_war_country = yes
							event_target:civwar_party = {
								every_owned_pop_group = {
									limit = { has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat }
									random = {
										chance = 80
										remove_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
									}
								}
							}
						}

					}
				}
				else_if = { # > 2 Sectors, if applicable, up to 67% can revolt
					limit = {
						num_sectors > 2
						any_owned_sector = {
							NOR = {
								any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
								any_owned_planet = {
									pop_amount > 0
									OR = {
										is_capital = yes
										has_ground_combat = yes
										is_occupied_flag = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					log = "| Multi Sector Civwar"
					
					export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
						trigger = num_sectors
						variable = all_potential_sectors
					}

					set_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_sectors value = 0 }

					every_owned_sector = { # Calculate the rebel percentage of each sector
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
								any_owned_planet = {
									pop_amount > 0
									OR = {
										is_capital = yes
										has_ground_combat = yes
										is_occupied_flag = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						set_sector_flag = potential_rebel
						export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
							trigger = pop_amount
							variable = total_sector_pops
						}
						set_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 0 }
						every_owned_planet = {
							every_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = {
									exists = pop_faction
									pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									rebel_happiness = yes
								}
								prevprev = { change_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 1 } }
							}
						}
						divide_variable = {
							which = fac_sep_percentage
							value = total_sector_pops
						}
						multiply_variable = { # Needs to be done to ensure accurate ordering
							which = fac_sep_percentage
							value = 100
						}
						prev = { change_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_sectors value = 1 } }
					}

					random_list = {
						21 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.3 } }
						31 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.4 } }
						46 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.5 } }
						2 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.67 } }
					}
					ceiling_variable = all_potential_sectors

					while = { # Pick the most separatist sectors to rebel
						count = all_potential_sectors
						ordered_owned_sector = {
							limit = {
								has_sector_flag = potential_rebel
								NOT = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector }
							}
							position = 0
							order_by = fac_sep_percentage
							set_sector_flag = rebel_sector
							log = "==============================="
							log = "REBEL SECTOR: [this.GetName]"
						}
					}

					while = { # Associate bordering sectors with each other
						count = all_potential_sectors
						if = {
							limit = {
								any_owned_sector = {
									has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
									NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
								}
							}
							ordered_owned_sector = {
								limit = {
									has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
									NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
								}
								position = 0
								order_by = fac_sep_percentage
								save_event_target_as = rebel_sector_leader
								set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@this
								set_sector_flag = leader_sector
								set_sector_flag = counted_sector
							}
							# log = "==============================="
							# log = "LEADER SECTOR: [rebel_sector_leader.GetName]"
							while = {
								count = all_potential_sectors
								if = { # Any Sector that borders a more rebellious sector will rebel under them
									limit = {
										any_owned_sector = {
											NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
											has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
											any_system_within_border = {
												closest_system = {
													limit = {
														exists = sector
														sector = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
													}
													use_bypasses = yes
													max_steps = 1
												}
											}
										}
									}
									random_owned_sector = {
										limit = {
											NOT = { has_sector_flag = counted_sector }
											has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
											any_system_within_border = {
												closest_system = {
													limit = {
														exists = sector
														sector = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
													}
													use_bypasses = yes
													max_steps = 1
												}
											}
										}
										set_sector_flag = counted_sector
										set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
										set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_follower_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
						set_recent_civwar_flag = yes
					}

					# I Imagine this should stay past the first loop
					event_target:civwar_party = {
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
						random_owned_pop_group = { species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species } }
					}

					# create_party_name_leader = yes

					set_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value = 0 }
					every_owned_sector = {
						limit = { has_sector_flag = leader_sector }
						prev = { change_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value = 1 } }
					}

					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value > 1 } }
						set_timed_country_flag = { flag = rebs_help_eachother months = 2 }
						random = { ## For multiple disparate sectors, this needs to be handled differently
							chance = value:total_war_chance
							ordered_owned_sector = {
								limit = { has_sector_flag = leader_sector }
								position = 0
								order_by = fac_sep_percentage
								set_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector
								save_event_target_as = rebel_sector_leader
							}
							every_owned_sector = { ## Get rid of other leader sectors
								limit = {
									has_sector_flag = leader_sector
									NOT = { has_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector }
								}
								remove_sector_flag = leader_sector
							}
							every_owned_sector = {
								limit = {
									NOT = { has_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector }
									has_sector_flag = rebel_sector
								}
								set_sector_flag = TW_counted_sector
								set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
								set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_follower_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader
							}
							event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { set_variable = { which = multi_sector_TW_trigger value = 1 } }
							make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = { event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { check_variable = { which = multi_sector_TW_trigger value = 1 } } }
						event_target:cw_scope_initializer = { clear_variable = multi_sector_TW_trigger }
					}
					else = {
						while = {
							count = total_reb_countries
							ordered_owned_sector = {
								limit = { has_sector_flag = leader_sector }
								position = 0
								order_by = fac_sep_percentage
								if = {
									limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
									make_civil_war_ethic_country = yes
								}
								else = { make_nat_war_country = yes }
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { which = total_reb_countries value > 1 } }
							last_created_country = { set_timed_country_flag = { flag = allied_via_@event_target:cw_scope_initializer.owner months = 2 } }
						}
					}
					
					every_owned_sector = {
						limit = { has_sector_flag = potential_rebel }
						remove_sector_flag = potential_rebel
					}

					if = {
						limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_nationalist_faction = yes } }
						event_target:civwar_party = {
							every_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = { has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat }
								random = {
									chance = 80
									remove_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = { # >= 2 Planets
					limit = {
						num_owned_planets >= 2
						any_owned_planet = {
							pop_amount > 0
							solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
							has_ground_combat = no
							is_occupied_flag = no
							# any_owned_pop_group = {
							# 	exists = pop_faction
							# 	pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
							# }
						}
					}

					event_target:civwar_party = {
						set_variable = { which = mil_support value = 0 }
						save_event_target_as = civwar_party
					}
					if = { # If only 2 planets, get the other planet
						limit = { num_owned_planets = 2 }
						log = "| 2 Planet Civil War"
						random_owned_planet = {
							limit = {
								pop_amount > 0
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
							}
							save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
							solar_system = { set_timed_star_flag = { flag = civwar_planet months = 3 } }
							if = {
								limit = { any_owned_pop_group = { exists = pop_faction pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party } } }
								every_owned_pop_group = {
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
										rebel_happiness = yes
									}
									event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
								}
								random_owned_pop_group = {
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
										rebel_happiness = yes
									}
									species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
								}
							}
							else = {
								event_target:civwar_party = {
									every_owned_pop_group = { event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } } }
									random_owned_pop_group = { species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species } }
								}
							}
							
							set_carrier_flag = small_civil_war
							if = {
								limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
								get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
							}
							else = {
								set_carrier_flag = nat_planet_rebellion_@this # Such that Balkanization doesnt result in a bunch of wars numbered different civil wars at the same time
								get_nat_plan_rebels = yes
							}
						}
					}
					else = { # If more than 2, potentially Balkanize
						set_variable = { which = total_happiness_pops value = 0 }
						every_owned_pop_group = {
							limit = { pop_group_has_happiness = yes }
							prev = { change_variable = { which = total_happiness_pops value = 1 } }
						}
						log = "| Potential Balkan Civil War"
						set_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_planets value = 0 }
						every_owned_planet = {
							limit = {
								pop_amount > 0
								solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
								has_ground_combat = no
								is_occupied_flag = no
								# any_owned_pop_group = {
								# 	exists = pop_faction
								# 	pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
								# }
							}
							set_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 0 }
							every_owned_pop_group = {
								limit = {
									exists = pop_faction
									pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									rebel_happiness = yes
								}
								prev = { change_variable = { which = fac_sep_percentage value = 1 } }
							}
							prev = { change_variable = { which = maybe_rebel_planets value = 1 } }
							divide_variable = {
								which = fac_sep_percentage
								value = prev.total_happiness_pops
							}
						}
						while = {
							count = maybe_rebel_planets
							ordered_owned_planet = {
								limit = {
									pop_amount > 0
									solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
									has_ground_combat = no
									is_occupied_flag = no
									# any_owned_pop_group = {
									# 	exists = pop_faction
									# 	pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									# }
								}
								position = 0
								order_by = fac_sep_percentage
								save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
								solar_system = { set_timed_star_flag = { flag = civwar_planet months = 3 } }
								every_owned_pop_group = {
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
										rebel_happiness = yes
									}
									event_target:civwar_party = { change_variable = { which = mil_support value = 1 } }
								}
								random_owned_pop_group = {
									limit = {
										exists = pop_faction
										pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
									}
									species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
								}
								set_carrier_flag = balkan_war
								
								if = {
									limit = { event_target:civwar_party = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
									set_carrier_flag = ethic_planet_rebellion_@this # Such that Balkanization doesnt result in a bunch of wars numbered different civil wars at the same time
									get_ethic_plan_rebels = yes
									set_recent_civwar_flag = yes
								}
								else = {
									set_carrier_flag = nat_planet_rebellion_@this # Such that Balkanization doesnt result in a bunch of wars numbered different civil wars at the same time
									get_nat_plan_rebels = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
					root = {
						if = {
							limit = { target = { is_regular_faction = yes } }
							situation_event = { id = civethic.9000 }
						}
						else = {
							situation_event = { id = civnat.9010 }
							target = {
								every_owned_pop_group = {
									limit = { has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat }
									random = {
										chance = 80
										remove_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		# else_if = {
		# 	limit = { root.target = { is_scope_type = leader } }
			
		# }
	}
	log = "--------------------------------"
}

get_heir_multi_sector_effect = {
	save_event_target_as = civwar_originator

	export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
		trigger = num_sectors
		variable = all_potential_sectors
	}

	export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
		trigger = num_sectors
		variable = all_potential_sectors
	}

	random_list = {
		45 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.3 } }
		35 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.4 } }
		15 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.5 } }
		5 = { multiply_variable = { which = all_potential_sectors value = 0.6 } }
	}

	ceiling_variable = all_potential_sectors

	random_owned_sector = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } } }
		save_event_target_as = rebel_sector_leader
		set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@this
		set_sector_flag = counted_sector
		set_sector_flag = leader_sector
	}

	while = {
		count = all_potential_sectors
		random_owned_sector = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes }
					has_sector_flag = counted_sector
				}
				any_system_within_border = {
					closest_system = {
						limit = {
							exists = sector
							sector = { has_sector_flag = rebel_sector_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
						}
						use_bypasses = yes
						max_steps = 1
					}
				}
			}
			sector = { set_sector_flag = rebel_sector_follower_@event_target:rebel_sector_leader }
			set_sector_flag = counted_sector
		}
	}
	create_heir_country_effect = yes	
}

get_heir_one_sector_effect = {
	save_event_target_as = civwar_originator
	# find and flag systems
	random_owned_sector = {
		limit = { NOT = { any_system_within_border = { is_capital_system = yes } } }
	    set_sector_flag = leader_sector 
	}
	create_heir_country_effect = yes		
}

create_heir_country_effect = {
	set_country_flag = heir_mother_country
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country = { is_imperial_authority = yes } }
		create_country = {
			authority = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country
			inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/heir_dummy_country }
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_country = {
			authority = auth_imperial
			inline_script = { script = civil_war_country_creation/heir_dummy_country }
		}
	}
}

get_heir_one_planet_effect = {
	save_event_target_as = civwar_originator

	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_planet = {
				is_capital = no
				solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
				any_owned_pop_group = { can_think = yes }
			}
		}
		random_owned_planet = {
			limit = {
				is_capital = no
				solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
				any_owned_pop_group = { can_think = yes }
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
			set_carrier_flag = heir_planetary_rebellion

			if = { # Idk why, but noticed this being an issue.
				# Will investigate eventually.
				# Dont know if it effects sector and civil, but added just to be safe.
				limit = { event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country = { is_imperial_authority = yes } }
				create_rebels = {
					name = random
					species = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler.species
					authority = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country
					ethos = random
					origin = origin_heir_rebellion
					civics = random
					flag = random
					effect = {
						set_country_flag = heir_rebel_country
						set_leader = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler
						owner_main_species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
						event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country = { destroy_country = yes }
						created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				create_rebels = {
					name = random
					species = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler.species
					authority = auth_imperial
					ethos = random
					origin = origin_heir_rebellion
					civics = random
					flag = random
					effect = {
						set_country_flag = heir_rebel_country
						set_leader = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler
						owner_main_species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
						event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country = { destroy_country = yes }
						created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}

			carrier_event = { id = civplan.9000 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_owned_planet = { is_capital = no solar_system = { is_capital_system = no } } }
		random_owned_planet = {
			limit = { is_capital = no solar_system = { is_capital_system = no } }
			
			save_event_target_as = civil_war_planet
			set_carrier_flag = heir_planetary_rebellion

			if = { # Idk why, but noticed this being an issue.
				# Will investigate eventually.
				# Dont know if it effects sector and civil, but added just to be safe.
				limit = { event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country = { is_imperial_authority = yes } }
				create_rebels = {
					name = random
					species = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler.species
					authority = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country
					ethos = random
					origin = origin_heir_rebellion
					civics = random
					flag = random
					effect = {
						set_country_flag = heir_rebel_country
						set_leader = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler
						owner_main_species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
						event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country = { destroy_country = yes }
						created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				create_rebels = {
					name = random
					species = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler.species
					authority = auth_imperial
					ethos = random
					origin = origin_heir_rebellion
					civics = random
					flag = random
					effect = {
						set_country_flag = heir_rebel_country
						set_leader = event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country.ruler
						owner_main_species = { save_event_target_as = civwar_species }
						event_target:heir_civil_war_dummy_country = { destroy_country = yes }
						created_civwar_rebel_country_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}

			carrier_event = { id = civplan.9000 }
		}
	}
}
