					owner = { NOT = { has_country_flag = destroyed_country_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country } }
				}
				random_list = {
					10 = { }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.1 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.2 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.3 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.4 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.5 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.6 } }
					5 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.7 } }
					5 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.8 } }
					3 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.9 } }
					2 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					20 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.1 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.2 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.3 } }
					13 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.4 } }
					12 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.5 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.6 } }
					5 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.7 } }
					5 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.8 } }
					3 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.9 } }
					2 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}

		while = {
			count = num_separatist_pops
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					pop_can_be_separatist_gen = yes
					has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
					has_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_full }
				}
				set_nat_modifier_effect = yes
				set_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
				set_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}
		}

		# Second, Get Citizens who've become residents
		set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0 }

		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				pop_can_be_separatist_gen = yes
				has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
				has_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_limited }
			}
			prev = { change_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value <= 10 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					owner = { NOT = { has_country_flag = destroyed_country_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country } }
				}
				random_list = {
					15 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
					15 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 2 } }
					15 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 3 } }
					15 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 4 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 5 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 6 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 7 } }
					5 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 8 } }
					3 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 9 } }
					2 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 10 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 2 } }
					15 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 3 } }
					15 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 4 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 5 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 6 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 7 } }
					10 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 8 } }
					5 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 9 } }
					5 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 10 } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value > 10 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					owner = { NOT = { has_country_flag = destroyed_country_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country } }
				}
				random_list = {
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.1 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.2 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.3 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.4 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.5 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.6 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.7 } }
					5 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.8 } }
					3 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.9 } }
					2 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.1 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.2 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.3 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.4 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.5 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.6 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.7 } }
					10 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.8 } }
					5 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.9 } }
					5 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}

		while = {
			count = num_separatist_pops
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					pop_can_be_separatist_gen = yes
					has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
					has_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_limited }
				}
				set_nat_modifier_effect = yes
				set_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
				set_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}
		}

		# Third, Get Citizens and Residents who've become enslaved
		set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0 }

		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				pop_can_be_separatist_gen = yes
				OR = {
					has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
					has_pop_group_flag = resident_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
				}
				has_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery }
			}
			prev = { change_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value <= 10 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					owner = { NOT = { has_country_flag = destroyed_country_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country } }
				}
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 7 } }
					30 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 8 } }
					20 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 9 } }
					15 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 10 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					25 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 7 } }
					25 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 8 } }
					25 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 9 } }
					25 = { set_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 10 } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value > 10 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					owner = { NOT = { has_country_flag = destroyed_country_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country } }
				}
				random_list = {
					35 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.7 } }
					30 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.8 } }
					20 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.9 } }
					15 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					25 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.7 } }
					25 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.8 } }
					25 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 0.9 } }
					25 = { multiply_variable = { which = num_separatist_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}

		while = {
			count = num_separatist_pops
			random_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = {
					pop_can_be_separatist_gen = yes
					OR = {
						has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
						has_pop_group_flag = resident_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
					}
					has_citizenship_type = { type = citizenship_slavery }
				}
				set_nat_modifier_effect = yes
				set_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat
				set_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country
				planet = {
					set_carrier_flag = sep_pop_nat
					set_carrier_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country
				}
			}
			
		}
	}

	# Finally, Get Citizens and Residents who are now being purged - all of them
	get_cit_res_purgees = yes
}

get_nat_change_flags = {
	event_target:nat_pops_country = {
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:old_nat_country = { has_country_flag = ancient_exile_gov } }
			set_country_flag = ancient_exile_gov
		}				
	}

	# establish comms
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_communications = event_target:old_nat_country }
		establish_communications_no_message = event_target:nat_pops_country
		establish_contact = { who = event_target:nat_pops_country location = root }
	}

	# establish comms
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_communications = event_target:old_nat_country }
		establish_communications_no_message = event_target:nat_pops_country
		establish_contact = { who = event_target:nat_pops_country location = root }
	}

	every_galaxy_planet = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_carrier_flag = capital_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
				has_carrier_flag = frontier_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
				has_carrier_flag = integrated_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
				has_carrier_flag = fundamental_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
				any_owned_pop_group = {
					OR = {
						has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:old_nat_country
						has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
						has_pop_group_flag = resident_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
						has_pop_group_flag = slave_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
						has_pop_group_flag = bio_trophy_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
						has_pop_group_flag = purgee_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_carrier_flag = capital_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country }
			remove_carrier_flag = capital_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
			set_carrier_flag = capital_planet_@event_target:nat_pops_country
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_carrier_flag = frontier_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country }
			remove_carrier_flag = frontier_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
			set_carrier_flag = frontier_planet_@event_target:nat_pops_country
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_carrier_flag = integrated_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country }
			remove_carrier_flag = integrated_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
			set_carrier_flag = integrated_planet_@event_target:nat_pops_country
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_carrier_flag = fundamental_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country }
			remove_carrier_flag = fundamental_planet_@event_target:old_nat_country
			set_carrier_flag = fundamental_planet_@event_target:nat_pops_country
		}

		random_galaxy_planet = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = owner } }
			save_event_target_as = dupe_planet
		}

		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:old_nat_country
					has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
					has_pop_group_flag = resident_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
					has_pop_group_flag = slave_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
					has_pop_group_flag = bio_trophy_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
					has_pop_group_flag = purgee_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:old_nat_country }
				remove_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:old_nat_country
				set_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:old_nat_country }
				remove_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
				set_pop_group_flag = citizen_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_pop_group_flag = resident_of_@event_target:old_nat_country }
				remove_pop_group_flag = resident_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
				set_pop_group_flag = resident_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_pop_group_flag = slave_of_@event_target:old_nat_country }
				remove_pop_group_flag = slave_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
				set_pop_group_flag = slave_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_pop_group_flag = bio_trophy_of_@event_target:old_nat_country }
				remove_pop_group_flag = bio_trophy_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
				set_pop_group_flag = bio_trophy_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_pop_group_flag = purgee_of_@event_target:old_nat_country }
				remove_pop_group_flag = purgee_of_@event_target:old_nat_country
				set_pop_group_flag = purgee_of_@event_target:nat_pops_country
			}

		}
	}
}

# Government in Exile

create_exile_enclave_country = {
	save_event_target_as = hosting_country
	set_country_flag = exile_already_asked_@from # So that if they release the GiE, they do not get asked to host again

	from = { save_event_target_as = old_nat_country }

	if = {
		limit = { any_owned_planet = { NOT = { has_carrier_flag = exiled_gov_host_planet } } }
		if = {
			limit = { capital_scope = { NOT = { has_carrier_flag = exiled_gov_host_planet } } }
			capital_scope = {
				set_carrier_flag = exiled_gov_host_planet
				save_event_target_as = gov_hosting_planet
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_carrier_flag = exiled_gov_host_planet } }
				set_carrier_flag = exiled_gov_host_planet
				save_event_target_as = gov_hosting_planet
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_system_within_border = {
			random_system_planet = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_carrier_flag = exiled_gov_host_planet } }
				set_carrier_flag = exiled_gov_host_planet
				save_event_target_as = gov_hosting_planet
			}
		}
	}

	event_target:old_nat_country = {
		set_country_type = enclave_exiled_gov
		save_event_target_as = nat_pops_country
		set_country_flag = exiled_gov_enclave_country
		create_fleet = {
			settings = { spawn_debris = no }
			effect = {
				set_owner = event_target:old_nat_country
				create_ship = {
					name = "Exiled Government Headquarters"
					design = "NAME_Mercenary_Enclave_Station_1"
					graphical_culture = event_target:old_nat_country
				}
				set_location = {
					target = event_target:gov_hosting_planet
					distance = 30
					angle = random
				}
				save_event_target_as = new_capital_station
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_secession_country_flag = yes }
			find_secession_number_flag = yes
		}
		set_custom_capital_location = event_target:gov_hosting_planet
		#Set ruler
		set_country_flag = has_host
		set_country_flag = host_is_@root
		# find_nat_number_flag = yes ## This shouldnt be necessary here, its the same target
		get_separatist_faction_nat = yes
		country_event = { id = civnat.1320 }
	}
	
	event_target:gov_hosting_planet = {
		set_carrier_flag = exile_enclave_link_@event_target:nat_pops_country
	}
}

get_restoration_planets = {
	set_variable = { which = total_restoration_planets value = 0 }

	every_owned_planet = {
		limit = {
			is_occupied_flag = no
			is_under_colonization = no
			has_orbital_bombardment = no
			solar_system = { is_capital_system = no }
		}
		set_carrier_flag = restoration_planet
		prev = { change_variable = { which = total_restoration_planets value = 1 } }
	}

	# Next, we save each subject as an Event Target
	# As the scripting language doesn't have proper loops, we use a lot of ifs if there are more than 10, the event can be run again.
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 3 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 4 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 7 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 8 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 9 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 10 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 11 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 12 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 13 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 14 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 15 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 16 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 17 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 18 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 19 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_planet_effect NUM = 20 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { any_owned_planet = { has_carrier_flag = restoration_planet } }
		every_owned_planet = {
			limit = { has_carrier_flag = restoration_planet }
			remove_carrier_flag = restoration_planet
		}
	}	
}

get_restoration_sectors = {
	set_variable = { which = total_restoration_sectors value = 0 }

	every_owned_sector = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { any_owned_planet = { is_capital = yes } }
			NOT = { any_owned_planet = { is_occupied_flag = yes } }
			NOT = { any_owned_planet = { has_orbital_bombardment = yes } }
		}
		set_sector_flag = restoration_sector
		prev = { change_variable = { which = total_restoration_sectors value = 1 } }
	}

	# Next, we save each subject as an Event Target
	# As the scripting language doesn't have proper loops, we use a lot of ifs if there are more than 10, the event can be run again.
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 3 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 4 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 7 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 8 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 9 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 10 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 11 }

	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 13 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 14 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 15 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 16 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 17 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 18 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 19 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/restoration_sector_effect NUM = 20 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { any_owned_sector = { has_sector_flag = restoration_sector } }
		every_owned_sector = {
			limit = { has_sector_flag = restoration_sector }
			remove_sector_flag = restoration_sector
		}
	}
}

exile_restore_planet_setup = {

	event_target:exiled_gov_enclave_country = { save_event_target_as = new_homeland }

	set_owner = event_target:new_homeland
	starbase = { set_owner = event_target:new_homeland }
	solar_system = {
		every_system_planet = {
			limit = { is_owned_by = root }
			set_owner = event_target:new_homeland
		}
	}
}

exile_restore_sector_setup = {

	event_target:exiled_gov_enclave_country = { save_event_target_as = new_homeland }

	every_owned_planet = { # Every system with planets
		set_owner = event_target:new_homeland
		starbase = { set_owner = event_target:new_homeland }
	}
	every_system_within_border = { # Every other system, changing this gets weird, so just leave it cause it works fine
		starbase = { set_owner = event_target:new_homeland }
	}
}

exile_restoration_gen_effect = {

	if = {
		limit = { event_target:exiled_gov_host = { is_same_value = root } }
		event_target:new_homeland = { set_country_flag = was_true_host }
	}
	else = {
		save_event_target_as = new_host
	}

	event_target:exiled_gov_host = { save_event_target_as = exile_host }

	event_target:new_homeland = {

		set_country_type = default

		# Destroy old enclave station
		random_owned_fleet = {
			limit = { any_owned_ship = { is_ship_size = enclave_mercenary_station } }
			destroy_fleet = this
		}

		if = { # If they don't have their original capital planet, they rename their empire.
			limit = { any_owned_planet = { has_carrier_flag = capital_planet_@event_target:new_homeland } }
			random_owned_planet = {
				limit = { has_carrier_flag = capital_planet_@event_target:new_homeland }
				set_capital = yes
			}
			set_origin = origin_restored_government
			create_leader = {
				class = random_ruler
				event_leader = yes
				hide_leader = yes
				species = owner_main_species
				name = random
				effect = {
					set_name = {
						key = dummy_old_name_leader
						variable_string = "\\[new_homeland.GetName]"
					}
					set_leader_flag = dummy_leader
					save_event_target_as = dummy_old_name_leader
				}
			}
			last_created_leader = { leader_event = { id = civ_misc_leader.1000 days = 180 } }
		}
		else_if = { # Ideally make the most habitable planet their capital, but idk if you can use the habitability trigger with order_by
			limit = {
				count_owned_planet = {
					count >= 2
					limit = { is_under_colonization = no }
				}
			}
			every_owned_planet = {
				limit = {
					is_under_colonization = no
					habitability = { who = owner_main_species value >= 0 }
				}
				export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
					trigger = habitability
					parameters = { who = owner_main_species }
					variable = new_planet_hab_percent
				}
			}
			ordered_owned_planet = {
				limit = { is_under_colonization = no }
				position = 0
				order_by = new_planet_hab_percent
				set_capital = yes
			}
			every_owned_planet = {
				limit = {
					is_under_colonization = no
					habitability = { who = owner_main_species value >= 0 }
				}
				clear_variable = new_planet_hab_percent
			}
			create_leader = {
				class = random_ruler
				event_leader = yes
				hide_leader = yes
				species = owner_main_species
				name = random
				effect = {
					set_name = {
						key = dummy_old_name_leader
						variable_string = "\\[new_homeland.GetName]"
					}
					set_leader_flag = dummy_leader
					save_event_target_as = dummy_old_name_leader
				}
			}
			last_created_leader = { leader_event = { id = civ_misc_leader.1000 days = 180 } }
			capital_scope.carrier = { set_name = random }
			set_name = random
			set_country_flag = nationalists_new_name
			set_origin = origin_restored_successor_government
		}
		else = {
			create_leader = {
				class = random_ruler
				event_leader = yes
				hide_leader = yes
				species = owner_main_species
				name = random
				effect = {
					set_name = {
						key = dummy_old_name_leader
						variable_string = "\\[new_homeland.GetName]"
					}
					set_leader_flag = dummy_leader
					save_event_target_as = dummy_old_name_leader
				}
			}
			last_created_leader = { leader_event = { id = civ_misc_leader.1000 days = 180 } }
			capital_scope.carrier = { set_name = random }
			set_name = random
			set_country_flag = nationalists_new_name
			set_origin = origin_restored_successor_government
		}

		# The nationalists in the restored homeland have won, they don't need to be nationalist anymore 
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@prev
				nats_modifier_flag_matcher = { SCOPE = prev }
			}
			remove_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@prev
		}

		# If nationalists elsewhere exist for a country that then is restored, make them happy and potentially resettle them
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = { has_pop_group_flag = sep_pop_nat_@event_target:new_homeland }
			add_modifier = { modifier = "restoration_of_motherland" years = 15 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_citizenship_rights = yes
					has_migration_control = { type = no }
				}
				random = {
					chance = 75
					resettle_pop_group = {
						POP_GROUP = this
						PLANET = event_target:new_homeland.capital_scope
						AMOUNT = 100
					}
				}
			}
		}

		# Science
		create_fleet = {
			effect = {
				set_owner = event_target:new_homeland
				create_ship = {
					name = random
					random_existing_design = science
				}
				set_fleet_stance = evasive				
				set_location = event_target:new_homeland.capital_scope
				owner = {
					get_leader_homeworld = yes
					create_leader = {
						name = random
						class = scientist
						sub_type = survey
						species = owner_main_species
						background_planet = event_target:leader_homeworld
					}
				}
				set_leader = last_created_leader
			}			
		}

		# Construction Ship
		create_fleet = {
			effect = {
				set_owner = event_target:new_homeland
				create_ship = {
					name = random
					random_existing_design = constructor
				}
				set_fleet_stance = evasive
				set_location = event_target:new_homeland.capital_scope
			}
		}

		# Create Leaders
		get_leader_homeworld = yes
		create_leader = {
			class = official
			species = owner_main_species
			name = random
			background_planet = event_target:leader_homeworld
		}
		while = {
			count = 2
			get_leader_homeworld = yes
			create_leader = {
				class = commander
				species = owner_main_species
				name = random
				background_planet = event_target:leader_homeworld
			}
		}
		while = {
			count = 3
			get_leader_homeworld = yes
			create_leader = {
				class = scientist
				species = owner_main_species
				name = random
				background_planet = event_target:leader_homeworld
			}
		}

		# Create some of their original species, as if relocating 
		capital_scope = {
			while = {
				count = 3
				create_pop_group = {
					species = owner_main_species
					ethos = random
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = was_true_host }
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				who = event_target:exile_host
				modifier = opinion_restored_nats
			}
			event_target:exile_host = {
				add_intel = {
					who = event_target:new_homeland
					amount = 70
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				who = event_target:new_host
				modifier = opinion_restored_nats
			}
			event_target:new_host = {
				add_intel = {
					who = event_target:new_homeland
					amount = 70
				}
			}
			set_truce = { target = event_target:new_host type = liberation }
		}
		set_truce = { target = event_target:exile_host type = liberation }

		# Set Country birth date
		country_event = { id = civmisc.3000 }

		# Give resources based on pops, needs to be delayed as pops dont change ownership instantly
		country_event = { id = civnat.1370 days = 2 }

		every_playable_country = {
			# limit = { NOT = { is_same_value = root } }
			country_event = { id = civnat.9205 }
		}
	}
}

######################
## Misc

get_remove_civics = {
	while = {
		limit = { civics_count >= 1 }
		force_remove_civic_by_index = 1
	}
}

reset_civics_effect = {
	change_government = { remove_invalid_civics = yes civics = random cooldown = no }
}

cw_shift_ethic_effect = {
	while = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_ethic = ethic_fanatic_$ETHIC$ } }
		shift_ethic = ethic_$ETHIC$
	}
}

get_ship_delete_effect = {
	every_owned_fleet = {
		set_variable = { which = ship_count value = 0 }
		every_owned_ship = {
			limit = { is_ship_class = shipclass_military }
			PREV = { change_variable = { which = ship_count value = $PERCENT$ } }
		}
		divide_variable = { which = ship_count value = 100 }
		while = {
			count = ship_count
			random_owned_ship = {
				limit = {
		    		is_ship_class = shipclass_military
		    		is_modded_ship = no
		    	}
		    	if = {
		    		limit = { exists = leader }
		    		leader = { unassign_leader = this }
		    	}
		    	delete_ship = THIS
			}
		}
		set_variable = { which = ship_count value = 0 }
	}
}

get_helper_subjects_effect = {
	# If there are more then 10 subjects, more should appear every time one is chosen

	set_variable = { which = helper_subjects value = 0 }

	# Count how many valid Subject States there are
	every_subject = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = subject_helps_overlord_@root
			NOT = {
				any_war = {
					OR = {
						using_war_goal = { type = wg_civwar owner = root }
						using_war_goal = { type = wg_natwar owner = root }
						using_war_goal = { type = wg_civwar_heir owner = root }
						using_war_goal = { type = wg_coup_successor owner = root }
						using_war_goal = { type = wg_coup_independent owner = root }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = helper_sub_country
		overlord = { change_variable = { which = helper_subjects value = 1 } }
	}

	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 3 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 4 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 5 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 6 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 7 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 8 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 9 }
	}
	if = {
		inline_script = { script = effects/helper_subject_effect NUM = 10 }
	}
}

		20 = { set_timed_pop_faction_flag = { flag = faction_rebelled years = 7 } }
		15 = { set_timed_pop_faction_flag = { flag = faction_rebelled years = 10 } }
	}
}

rebel_army_effect = {
	set_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 0 }

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_carrier_flag = balkan_war
				has_carrier_flag = small_civil_war
				has_carrier_flag = nat_planet_rebellion_@this
				has_carrier_flag = ethic_planet_rebellion_@this
			}
			NOT = { has_carrier_flag = under_one_rule_small_civil_war }
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				exists = pop_faction
				pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
			}
			root = { change_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_carrier_flag = sep_sector_rebellion
				has_carrier_flag = nat_sector_rebellion
				has_carrier_flag = ethic_sector_rebellion
			}
		}
		sector = {
			every_owned_planet = {
				every_owned_pop_group = {
					limit = {
						exists = pop_faction
						pop_faction = { is_same_value = event_target:civwar_party }
					}
					root = { change_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_carrier_flag = heir_planetary_rebellion }
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = { can_think = yes }
			random = {
				chance = 35
				root = { change_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 1 } }
			}	
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_carrier_flag = plan_slave_revolt }
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				is_enslaved_or_purged = yes
				happiness < 0.6
			}
			root = { change_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 1 } }	
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_carrier_flag = sec_slave_revolt }
		sector = {
			every_owned_planet = {
				every_owned_pop_group = {
					limit = {
						is_enslaved_or_purged = yes
						happiness < 0.6
					}
					root = { change_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_carrier_flag = under_one_rule_small_civil_war }
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = { is_egalitarian = yes }
			root = { change_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 5 } }	
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_carrier_flag = sep_planet_rebellion }
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = { happiness <= 0.5 }
			root = { change_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 1 } }	
		}
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 0 } }
		log = "| NOTE: BACKUP SEPS CREATED"
		set_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = event_target:cw_scope_initializer.mil_support }
		# set_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 10 }
		# divide_variable = { which = num_sep_pops value = 3 }
		ceiling_variable = num_sep_pops
		log = "[this.num_sep_pops]"
	}

	log = "PLAN [event_target:civwar_rebels.GetName]"


	while = {
		count = num_sep_pops
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				create_civwar_army = { TYPE = assault NUM = 3 }
			}
			30 = {
				create_civwar_army = { TYPE = assault NUM = 2 }
				create_civwar_army = { TYPE = defense NUM = 2 }
			}
			20 = {
				create_civwar_army = { TYPE = assault NUM = 3 }
				create_civwar_army = { TYPE = defense NUM = 2 }
			}
		}
	}
}

create_civwar_army = {
	while = {
		count = $NUM$
		create_army = {
			name = random
			owner = event_target:civwar_rebels
			species = event_target:civwar_rebels.species
			type = civwar_$TYPE$_army
		}
	}
}

remove_warring_subjects = {
	every_war_participant = {
		limit = {
			has_overlord = root
			is_at_war_with = root
		}
		prev = { remove_war_participant = prev }
	}
}

######

set_fled_fled_year = {
	event_target:defeated_traitor = {
		set_variable = { which = year_fled value = 2200 }
		export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
			trigger = years_passed
			variable = years_passed_leader_fled
		}
		change_variable = { which = year_fled value = this.years_passed_leader_fled }
	}
}

kill_gov_switch_country = {
	if = {
		limit = { exists = event_target:gov_switch_country }
		event_target:gov_switch_country = { destroy_country = yes }
	}	
}
